(2) With flip=True, "her" is labelled as the parent, which assumes the parent is female; names don't carry gender. (3) "daughter ... her" is truly ambiguous, yet it gets both labels by coin flip. Anyone using O0b must drop this family or relabel it as ASK. The listener's converter drops it (scripts/claude_lis300_data.py).

- **15:35 228 determinism (Opus): PASS verified. The flake is found and fixed.** Cause: fix170's _src_of() cache keys a notebook's triples list by id() and never keeps the list alive. A freed address gets reused by rewrite_question's copy, so the rewrite check reads an old notebook's facts and drops the rewrite, and the reply becomes a decline or "I have no opinions." It needs earlier notebooks in the same process, which is why items run alone never flip. Fix: a 65-line identity guard (scripts/claude_fix228_srcguard.py). Seal 6/6 OK; only RESULTS.md was written after it. My recount: forced plant on 138i gives 60/202 donor hits and 0 rewrites; on 228, 0 hits and 60 rewrites. Bench under load x3: 0/0/0 moves. rt136/rt143/sessions152: 0 moves. Sleep smoke passes. Independent check sent to the verifier. **Decision:** 228 goes FIRST into the next merge. Once the verifier confirms, new experiments include SrcGuardMixin228 in their agent (base = 138i+228 guard; behaviour-neutral, 0 moves on every suite).

- **95 diagnosis of the ears47 FAIL (CPU re-inference reproduced every sealed panel exactly):** three separate problems. (1) The SEEN execution bar was unreachable by construction: 163 of 817 SEEN statements have OPEN gold and can never execute, so the max is 654 < bar 736; the model executed 455/654. (2) The 2 SAFE silent writes ("aside" leftovers) passed every brake — no brake checks unconsumed leftover words; tau 0.8766 came from ONE calibration item of ONE seed. (3) WEB is a confidence collapse on long text, not a missing family: web-positive sentences median 134 chars vs calibration 35 (MAX_LEN 96); 1,494/1,806 ensemble confidence < 0.3; relation match 38.8 %. Temperatures exonerated. A plain LTT grid would add ~350 silent wrong writes → rejected. Seal OK. → 118 (leftover brake, CPU) + 119 (length coverage, GPU after the talker). 109's calibration-only wave is dropped
